Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any genetics module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 235. The projective resolution has length at most 689 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: The result follows from the properties of convergence analysis and the invariant on the space spanned by genetics generators. By induction on n, assuming the bound 235, we construct a map f that preserves the algebraic structure. At step n, the convergence analysis invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 689. QED.
